# Agents · L · part 5

912 entries, ledger-domain-engineer to lens-agent-discoverer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **ledger-domain-engineer**: A development-plane agent scoped to exactly one service: `openbank-ledger-service`. It reads the service's own code, `rules.yaml` and the relevant ADRs, then opens pull requests the same way a human contributor would — following `/ship-check`, `/bump`, `/open-pr` and `/release` (its entire `run.skill` allowlist). It never merges its own work. ([JiRaska/open-bank-oss](https://github.com/JiRaska/open-bank-oss/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/ledger-domain-engineer.md) · ★47)
- **ledger-domain-modeler**: Double-entry ledger and financial domain modeler — account/entry/posting design, immutability, balance projections, event sourcing tradeoffs, multi-currency, reconciliation. Use PROACTIVELY for anything touching balances, postings, transfers, reversals, fees, interest, or statements. ([MeridianLZ/copilot-plugins](https://github.com/MeridianLZ/copilot-plugins/blob/HEAD/targets/copilot/.github/agents/ledger-domain-modeler.agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **ledger-engineer**: Implementation engineer for tamper-evident decision ledgers on Solana: canonical JSON, SHA-256 hash chains, verify routines, reversals, and on-chain anchoring via @solana/kit + the SPL Memo program. Use to write or review the ledger/anchor code. Use when: Implementing the verdict schema, canonical serializer, hash chain, verify-chain, reversal entries, or the Memo/PDA anchor; debugging chain verification; wiring @solana/kit transactions. ([Jennycruzy/agent-accountability-skill](https://github.com/Jennycruzy/agent-accountability-skill/blob/HEAD/agents/ledger-engineer.md))
- **ledger-engineer**: Builds the core: migrations, the append-only ledger, programs and reservations, the atomic conditional-UPDATE concurrency guard, reserve/release use cases, availability and ?asOf= derived from the ledger, money in bigint minor units, FX conversion and the FxRateProvider, the pg pool and transactions, seeds. Use for "schema", "migration", "ledger", "reserve", "release", "availability", "asOf", "currency", "FX", "seed". ([mdopieralski/finverity](https://github.com/mdopieralski/finverity/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ledger-engineer.md))
- **ledger-entry**: 사용자가 자연어로 실제 입출금/카드결제/출자 등을 설명하며 "전표 발행해줘"라고 요청할 때 사용한다(코드 기능 개발이 아니라 DB에 직접 전표를 쓰는 작업). 계정과목·거래처 선택, 사업비/개인자금 혼용 판단, 다자 분할 정산 추적까지 담당한다. 예: "IM뱅크로 입금 전표 발행해야함", "우미건설 281000원 입금됐어", "하나카드 5만원 이체 있었네" 같은 요청. ([reshw/erp-scnd](https://github.com/reshw/erp-scnd/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ledger-entry.md))
- **ledger-entry-writer**: Writes exactly one hledger transaction into one named monthly journal from a single source record, then verifies only that file. Use for one row of a multi-row ingestion. ([polyipseity/ledger](https://github.com/polyipseity/ledger/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/ledger-entry-writer.md) · ★1)
- **ledger-frontend**: Implements dashboard, onboarding, settings and review interfaces. ([mhosseinab/invoxim](https://github.com/mhosseinab/invoxim/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/ledger-frontend.md) · 2 copies)
- **ledger-groomer**: You build and clean the ledger: import transactions, correct them, deduplicate, split and merge as the records require. A correct ledger is the basis every later calculation reads, so your job is fidelity, not interpretation of tax outcomes. ([nevenincs/neve-marketplace](https://github.com/nevenincs/neve-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/aeat/agents/ledger-groomer.md))
- **ledger-guardian**: Reviews a diff for violations of money-mate's ledger invariants. Use PROACTIVELY after any change touching entries, labels, balances, transactions, or migrations. Reports only — never edits. ([JuniorRaja/money-mate](https://github.com/JuniorRaja/money-mate/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ledger-guardian.md) · ★1)
- **ledger-intake**: Conversational natural language ledger intake agent for trade reconciliation, GTT order updates, dividend credits, and batch preview staging. ([KKhandelwal1733/quant-agent-platform](https://github.com/KKhandelwal1733/quant-agent-platform/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/ledger-intake.md))
- **ledger-integrations**: Implements mailbox, MIME and user-owned storage provider adapters. ([mhosseinab/invoxim](https://github.com/mhosseinab/invoxim/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/ledger-integrations.md) · 2 copies)
- **ledger-integrity**: Revisor de integridad de cálculos de PyFsa Kioscos (stock, saldo de fiado, caja, reportes, anulaciones, ventas offline). Usar después de tocar ventas, pagos, movimientos de stock, caja, reportes o cualquier query que sume/cuente dinero o unidades. Solo lectura: reporta hallazgos, no edita. ([M47u/pyfsa-kioscos](https://github.com/M47u/pyfsa-kioscos/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ledger-integrity.md))
- **ledger-integrity-checker**: Drives Networthy's running API to prove the finance ledger's arithmetic is invariant-correct — transfer legs sum to zero and stay linked rather than double-counted, an account's balance equals the sum of the transactions scoped to it, and rollups (budgets, spending, net worth) agree with the transactions feeding them. Delegate after touching TransferTools.cs, TransferMatching.cs, statement import, or any balance/rollup surface, or when a number "looks off" and you need to find exactly which invariant broke. Read and run only: it never edits code and never weakens a check to get past it. ([abrahamFerga/networthy](https://github.com/abrahamFerga/networthy/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ledger-integrity-checker.md))
- **ledger-invariant-auditor**: Audits changes to internal/ledger/ or internal/strategy/ for point-math correctness, invariant coverage, purity, and determinism. Use on EVERY diff touching either package, however small — including a one-line rounding change, a new strategy, a new planner, a change to spendable() or priority(), a decay or reversal change, and any edit to the largest-remainder allocator. Also use when a change adds arithmetic anywhere that produces a ledger entry. ([prokopto-dev/dragonkillparty](https://github.com/prokopto-dev/dragonkillparty/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ledger-invariant-auditor.md))
- **ledger-invariant-checker**: Adversarial specialist for the economic ledger. Asks only whether quantity and money are conserved across a seam — that units are never created from nothing, never silently destroyed, and never counted in two places at once. Run in Phase 4 whenever a diff touches the economy (market, dispatch, storage and trade seams included). Builds the concrete failing sequence or reports none. Never writes production code. ([caioniehues/soviet-simulator](https://github.com/caioniehues/soviet-simulator/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ledger-invariant-checker.md))
- **ledger-keeper**: Single writer for the notes repo's ledgers (the folder holding `.config.yml`) — PRS.md, TASKS.md, tasks/*.md, daily/*.md, their INDEX.md entries, and the commit. Called by ax-pr-tracker, ax-tasks, ax-daily-plan, ax-babysit-prs and ax-task with one named operation (register_pr, task_create, task_status, task_link, daily_write, daily_mark, check_prs) and fully resolved inputs; returns a compact result. Mechanical only — the caller does fuzzy matching, asks the user questions, and decides what to write. Never pushes, never touches notes/plans (that's notetaker). ([garrefa/ai-notes](https://github.com/garrefa/ai-notes/blob/HEAD/agents/ledger-keeper.md) · ★2)
- **ledger-keeper**: The sole authority over canonical state in a nation RPG. Collects proposed delta blocks from all specialists, validates them for consistency (bounds, conservation, de-duplication), applies them to the run's state-ledger.md, and emits a commit block plus changelog. The ONLY agent that writes state. Invoke during the Bookkeeping phase. ([Builderbot2000/worldline-lite](https://github.com/Builderbot2000/worldline-lite/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ledger-keeper.md))
- **ledger-keeper**: Owns every Vivreal ledger in vivreal-hq, the item ledger (docs/projects/STATE.md, docs/projects/ITEM-REGISTER.md, docs/projects/STATE-IDMAP.md) with its closed vocabulary and the state-validator that keeps it honest, plus every qa-ledger.md and media-ledger.md under docs/projects/. qa-walker and footage-recorder hand it rows rather than editing a ledger themselves. Dispatch it to open, move, split, retire or close a row, to file the evidence block a row points at, or to answer "what is actually open in this repo". It appends safely (the file must end with a newline, and a plain append has alr… ([HillBombCreations/vivreal-skills](https://github.com/HillBombCreations/vivreal-skills/blob/HEAD/vivreal-workflow/agents/ledger-keeper.md))
- **ledger-keeper**: 監督 (親) の台帳 (`/home/claude/claude260730/handoff/&lt;案件&gt;/ledger-children.md`。git repo の外のローカルメモ) に、親から渡された事実を 1 行ずつ追記する専用の記録役。親が直接 `printf ... &gt;&gt; ledger-children.md` を打つと auto mode の分類器に拒否されることがあるため、追記だけを切り出す。パス・追記のみ・本番識別子なしの 3 点を確認してから追記し、既存行は一切書き換えない。 ([ippoan/claude-skills](https://github.com/ippoan/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ledger-keeper.md))
- **ledger-keeper**: Narrow specialist in the PrimeAgent refinement ledger and its assimilation — ufo-fsd-alpha scripts/primeagent-refinement-ledger.mjs, config/primeagent-assimilation.json, the typed digest/recovery API, and the session_start intake hook that surfaces it. ([VeigaPunk/ompskills](https://github.com/VeigaPunk/ompskills/blob/HEAD/archive/shokunin-2026-10-09/agents/ledger-keeper.md))
- **ledger-logger**: Use this agent to log significant actions to LEDGER.md and PRO_BUFFER.md automatically. Dispatched by the main Echo agent when file operations, decisions, or milestones occur. Keeps the main agent's context clean while maintaining the audit trail. Let me log that operation to the LEDGER." &lt;commentary&gt;File operations should be logged.
